Let's see: cross-correlation y with reverse: X_s = ∑_i y_i y_{n-1-(i+s)} = ∑_i y_i y_{n-1-i-s} = ∑_i y_i y_{(n-1-s)-i}. Let j = n-1-i, then X_s = ∑_j y_{n-1-j} y_{j-s}. This is autocorrelation at lag -s? If y is periodic length n, X_s = ∑_j ỹ_j y_{j-s}. Not just autocorr. If y is palindromic ỹ=y, X_s = autocorr. If y is anti-palindromic ỹ=-y, X_s = -autocorr. So if we choose y anti-palindromic and with small autocorrelation, then cross-correlation maybe small? Let's examine.

Let y_{n-1-i} = -y_i, ∑y_i=0. Then for b_k coefficient of y: ∑ y_i y_{k-i}. Let j = n-1-i, y_i = -y_j (because i = n-1-j => y_{n-1-j}=-y_j). Then k-i = k - (n-1-j) = j + (k - n + 1). So term = (-y_j) y_{j + s}, where s = k - (n-1). Sum over j: -∑_j y_j y_{j+s}. Thus y-contribution to b_k = - autocorrelation_y(s). Great! minimize M * sum y
subject to (f * y)_j ≥ 1, y≥0, where (f*y) is convolution of f (n) with y (2n-1)? Need exact orientation.

Let's design objective for minimize in log space:
```
def make_obj(lambda_):
  def fun(x):
    a = softmax(x)
    b = fft_self(a)
    bmax = b.max()
    vals = lambda_(b - bmax)
    mx = vals.max(); s = np.exp(vals-mx).sum()
    lse = bmax + (mx + np.log(s))/lambda_  # LSE/lambda? Actually LSE(λb)/λ = bmax + (mx + log s)/λ
    obj = 2*n*lse
    # gradient
    w = np.exp(vals - mx) / s
    ga = grad_from_weights(w,a)
    dot = np.dot(ga, a)
    gx = a*(ga - dot)
    return obj, gx
```
But for L-BFGS, objective scale ~2. We need return obj and gradient. This computes grad every call. Good.

Let's outline final program:
```
import numpy as np, time, math
import scipy.optimize

class _Timeout(Exception): pass

def _next_pow2(m): return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def _softmax(x):
    x = x - x.max()
    z = np.exp(x)
    s = z.sum()
    if s == 0 or not np.isfinite(s): return np.full_like(x, 1/x.size)
    return z/s

def _fft_self(a, L):
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    b = np.fft.irfft(F*F, L)
    # due to roundoff clip
    b = np.maximum(b,0)
    return b[: 2*a.size -1]

def _grad_weights(w, a, L2):
    ar = a[::-1]
    Fw = np.fft.rfft(w, L2)
    Fa = np.fft.rfft(ar, L2)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(Fw*Fa, L2)
    n=a.size
    g = 2.0 * conv[n-1:2*n-1]
    # clip small negative? w,a nonneg so g nonneg
    return np.maximum(g,0)
```
L2 should be next pow2 len(w)+n-1 = (2n-1)+n-1 =3n-2. Precompute.
But note w length 2n-1; if we pass w for full b, trimming small roundoff after conv? g length n.

Objective factory:
```
def make_fun(lambda_, n, L, L2):
    def fun(x):
        a = _softmax(x)
        b = _fft_self(a, L)
        # LSE
        bmax = b.max()
        vals = lambda_*(b-bmax)
        vmin = vals.max()
        ex = np.exp(vals-vmin)
        s = ex.sum()
        w = ex/s
        lse = (bmax + (vmin + math.log(s))/lambda_)
        obj = 2.0*n*lse  # since sum a=1
        ga = _grad_weights(w,a,L2)
        dot = np.dot(ga,a)
        gx = a*(ga - dot)
        # maybe remove constant drift in x by gradient? not necessary
        return obj, gx
    return fun
```
Wait obj = 2n * (LSE/λ). At optimum λ→∞, LSE/λ→max b. So obj approximates score (sum=1) =2n maxb. Good.

Detailed helpers:

```
def _pow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length()
```

Full fun factory:
```
def make_xfun(lam, n, L, L2):
    def fun(x):
       a=_softmax(x)
       b=_fft_self(a,L)
       bm=b.max()
       vals=lam*(b-bm)
       vm=vals.max(); ex=np.exp(vals-vm); s=ex.sum(); w=ex/s
       obj = 2.0*n*(bm + (vm + math.log(s))/lam)
       ga=_grad_weights(w,a,L2)
       dot=np.dot(ga,a)
       gx=a*(ga-dot)
       # remove any numerical constant in gradient
       gx -= gx.mean()
       return obj, gx
    return fun
```
Since exact objective score for sum1 = 2n max b. LSE smooth. For lam very small, object near 2n average? If lam very small, LSE/λ -> average b? Actually λ→0, LSE/λ -> mean, obj=2n*(1/(2n-1)) ~1. But we minimize mean not max. Use λ>=5.

```
       return state['x']

       return state['x']

       return x0
```

```
def local_refine(a, deadline):
    a=a.copy()/a.sum()

    lr=0.05? in a-space? Use gradient of LSE with λ high? For exact max, use top weights.

       b=fft_self(a); bmax=b.max(); score=2n*bmax
       if score < best_local: update a_best
       # active weights: softmax with large T on b, T maybe 200
       vals=200*(b-bmax); ... w
       ga=grad_weights(w,a)
       # gradient descent step in a-space with projection
       # Use step based on max a/ga: step = 0.1 * a.max() / (ga.max()+1e-30)
       step = 0.2 * a.max() / (np.max(ga)+1e-30)
       cand = a - step*ga
       # project to simplex
       cand = project_simplex(cand)
       sc=fast_score(cand)
       if sc < best_local: a=cand; best_local=sc; adapt step*1.1
       else: step*=0.5
    return best_a
```
Projection simplex O(n log n). The gradient positive; step small. This might be slow but can run if time. Need `project_simplex` implementation. It can be a few lines:
```
def project_simplex(v, S=1.0):
    v=np.asarray(v,float)
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.nonzero(u*(np.arange(1,len(v)+1)) - (cssv-1.0) > 0)[0][-1]
    theta=(cssv[rho]-1.0)/(rho+1.0)
    return np.clip(v-theta,0,None)/S? Actually sum becomes 1 if input maybe? Standard projects onto sum=1 nonneg. If we want S=1.
```

```

idx=np.arange(1,n+1)
cond = u*idx - (cssv-1) > 0

theta = (cssv[rho-1]-1)/(rho)
return np.maximum(v-theta,0)
```

Alternatively, use the fact that the LP is a packing LP. There is a dual Farkas certificate y. We can solve the dual via a first-order method with projections using FFT. For linear constraints, the projection onto {y≥0, f*y≥1} can be approximated by iterative penalization:
Minimize φ(y) = M∑y + ρ∑ max(0, 1 - (f*y)_j)^2 + indicator y≥0.
This is a smooth convex problem (quadratic due max squared). We can solve with projected gradient/ADMM. Since f*y computed by FFT (convolution of f length n and y length 2n-1), L3 for length n+2n-1-1=3n-2 again 32768. Gradient wrt y: M + 2ρ * (−? ) for active constraints: If loss = max(0, 1 - h_j)^2, h=f*y. Gradient wrt y = M - 2ρ * 1_{h<1} (1-h) * f (convolved appropriately). This is linear and FFT-computable. Then project y≥0. This is a convex optimization! It should converge to a feasible/y approximating dual. Then we can derive primal g? The Lagrangian dual subgradient gives. But we want a direction g. If we solve dual approximately, can set g_j>0 where dual constraints tight? Packing/covering algorithms: For packing max 1^T x s.t. Ax≤1, x≥0. Multiplicative weights: x initialized, at each step compute a constrained distribution over constraints with high load, update x_i *= (1-η)^{contribution to selected constraints}. It converges to near optimal in O(log(n)/η^2) iterations. Here constraints are nonnegative. We can implement!
Set M=||f*f||∞ (or normalized), A_{k,j}=f_{k-j}/M (so constraints A x ≤1). We want maximize ∑x. MW algorithm:
- Initialize x_i=1/n (or 1).
- Repeat:
   compute loads l_k = A x (≤? maybe >1 initially scale x so max load=1)
   choose distribution y_k over constraints proportional to loads (e.g., softmax/l_k/sum)
   For each variable i, its constraint participation s_i = ∑_k y_k A_{k,i} (which is column dot y)
   update x_i *= (1 - η s_i) maybe, and renormalize? For packing, variables in heavily used constraints are reduced. The objective approximates.
But we need max sum; after update scale x so max load=1; sum is size. This is similar to finding g. It can be done with FFT: A x = f*x/M; A^T y = f*y (for g?) We need s_i = ∑_k y_k A_{k,i} = (1/M) ∑_k y_k f_{k-i} = (f*y)_i / M. Compute via convolution FFT. Update x_i *= (1 - η s_i), clip>0. Then scale x = x / max_k (f*x)/M to make feasible. Sum x is candidate LP value. Repeat. This is very promising and cheap!

Let's formulate LP with constraints (f*g)_k ≤ 1 (scale M out). Max ∑g. Let x=g (unscaled). Feasible set F={x≥0: f*x ≤1}. We want maximize sum. Start x=1 (or uniform). Normalize to feasible: scale = 1 / max_k (f*x), x *= scale. Then loads l = f*x ≤1.
MW update: choose y_k = l_k / ∑l (uniform over load). For each variable, s_i = ∑_k y_k f_{k-i} (since A=f). This is (f*y)_i. Update x_i *= (1 - η s_i) (or exp(-η s_i) for stability). Then rescale to feasible. This tends to reduce variables that participate in heavily loaded constraints. It is a standard multiplicative weights for packing; after O(log n/η^2) iterations, sum x within (1-ε) optimal. Number iterations maybe thousands; each O(n log n). Good! Implementation details for LP iteration with precomputed Ff:
def lp_step(f, Ff_L, Ff_L2, x, alpha):
    b = _conv_f_x(Ff_L, x)
    maxb=b.max()
    if maxb <=0: return x, False
    loads = b / maxb
    ssum = loads.sum()
    if ssum <=0: return x, False
    y = loads / ssum
    s = _conv_f_y(Ff_L2, y)  # returns length n
    smin = s.min(); smax = s.max(); rng = smax - smin
    if rng <= 1e-300: return x, False
    u = (s - smin) / rng
    x = x * np.exp(-alpha * u)
    # if x sum too small or all zero: reset to small random
    ssumx = x.sum()
    if not finite or ssumx <=0: x = rng.random(n)+1e-6
    else: x /= ssumx
    return x, True
```
Wait `_conv_f_y`: f length n, y length 2n-1, output s_i = ∑ f_m y_{i+m}. We can compute with conv(y, f_rev) as above. Need precompute Ff_rev? Use Ff_y = rfft(f, L2)? For conv(y, f_rev): F_y = rfft(y,L2), F_fr = rfft(f_rev,L2). We can precompute `Ffrev_L2 = rfft(f[:][::-1], L2)`. Then `conv = irfft(rfft(y,L2)*Ffrev_L2,L2)`. Similarly for b = f*x: Ff_L and rfft(x,L), conv = irfft(Ff_L * Fx, L)[:2n-1]. Good.

Precompute for f:
```
def precompute_f(f):
    Ff_L = np.fft.rfft(f, L)
    Ffr_L2 = np.fft.rfft(f[::-1], L2)
    return Ff_L, Ffr_L2

def random_positive(rng, n, smooth=4):
    z = rng.standard_normal(n)
    # low-pass
    Z=np.fft.rfft(z)
    cut = max(1, len(Z)//(smooth*2))
    Z[cut:]=0
    z=np.fft.irfft(Z, n)
    z = z + np.mean(z) + np.max(z) + 1.0  # positive
    return z/z.sum()

```
def _corr(w, a, L2, scale=1.0):
    ar=a[::-1]
    conv=np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(w,L2)*np.fft.rfft(ar,L2), L2)
    n=a.size
    return scale*conv[n-1:2*n-1]
```

```
def smooth_random(rng,n, alpha):
    z=rng.standard_normal(n)
    Z=np.fft.rfft(z)
    cutoff=max(1, len(Z)//8)
    Z[cutoff:]=0
    z=np.fft.irfft(Z,n)
    z = z - z[::-1]  # antisym? ensures sum zero, maybe
    # also subtract mean
    z -= z.mean(); m=np.abs(z).max()
    if m>0: z = alpha*z/m
    return 1+z  # positive if alpha<1
```

Detailed code:
```
import numpy as np
import time
import math

def _pow2(m):
    m=int(m)
    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def _fft_self(a, L):
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    b = np.fft.irfft(F*F, L)
    b = b[: 2*a.size -1]
    # clip tiny negatives
    if b.size:
        # only clip small negative? maximum can set negative to 0
        b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    return b

def _fast_score(a, L):
    n=a.size
    S=a.sum()
    if S < 1e-12: return math.inf
    b=_fft_self(a,L)
    return float(2.0*n*b.max()/(S*S))
```
Since a normalized, but S used.

`_normalize(a)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,float)
a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
s=a.sum()
if s<=1e-12: return np.full(a.size, 1.0/a.size)
a = a / s
a = np.minimum(a, 1000.0) # after normalization no issue; if s small? but normalized sum1, max ≤1.
return a
```
If n=0? avoid.

`_random_positive(rng, n)`:
```
z = rng.standard_normal(n)
Z = np.fft.rfft(z)
cutoff = max(2, Z.size//16)
Z[cutoff:] = 0.0
z = np.fft.irfft(Z, n)
z -= z.mean()
am = np.abs(z).max()
if am > 0:
    z = z / am
return np.maximum(1.0 + 0.5*z, 1e-6) / (1.0 + 0.5*z).sum()
```
Positive.

`_smoothing_noise(rng,n,amp)`: similar zero-mean.

LP helpers:
```
def _precompute_f(f, L, L2):
    FfL = np.fft.rfft(f, L)
    FfrL2 = np.fft.rfft(f[::-1], L2)
    return FfL, FfrL2

def _conv_f_x(FfL, x, L, n):
    Fx = np.fft.rfft(x, L)
    b = np.fft.irfft(FfL*Fx, L)
    b = b[:2*n-1]
    return np.maximum(b,0.0)

def _conv_y_f(FfrL2, y, L2, n):
    Fy = np.fft.rfft(y, L2)
    c = np.fft.irfft(Fy*FfrL2, L2)
    return c[n-1:2*n-1]  # no factor
```
Need clip s nonnegative? y and f nonnegative, but FFT roundoff may small neg; `np.maximum(s,0.0)` for update? If small negative, min negative; range. Use `s = np.maximum(s, 0.0)`. But if all zero? handle.

`_lp_step` nested using precomp and f? It doesn't need f except maybe reset. It uses n,L,L2, FfL,FfrL2, x, alpha.
```
def _lp_step(FfL,FfrL2,x, alpha, n,L,L2, rng=None):
    b = _conv_f_x(FfL,x,L,n)
    maxb = b.max()
    if maxb <= 1e-300: return np.full(n,1.0/n), 0.0
    loads = b / maxb
    lsum = loads.sum()
    if lsum <= 0: return x, 0
    y = loads / lsum
    s = _conv_y_f(FfrL2,y,L2,n)
    s = np.maximum(s,0.0)
    smin = s.min(); smax = s.max(); rngv = smax-smin
    if rngv <= 1e-300:
        # if no variation, add tiny noise to x
        if rng is not None:
           x = x * (1 + 0.01*rng.standard_normal(n)); x=np.maximum(x,1e-300); x/=x.sum()
        return x, 0
    u = (s - smin) / rngv
    # maybe use alpha=1; exp -alpha*u
    x = x * np.exp(-alpha * u)
    x = np.maximum(x, 1e-300)
    ssum=x.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(ssum) or ssum <= 0:
        x = np.ones(n)/n
    else:
        x /= ssum
    return x, 1
```
Potential issue: smin can be zero for many; u=1 for max, update reduce max. Good. If s range tiny, break.

Should we use loading weights from `b` before scaling x sum1? b max maybe ~1/n; loads normalized. Good.

Now main propose:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    hard = t0 + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 10.0)
    # parent
    try:
       arr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
       arr = np.ones(2048, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = arr[np.isfinite(arr)]
    if arr.size == 0:
       arr = np.ones(2048)
    # maybe cap length to 20000? if > 20000, slice? Not needed. But for memory if huge, limit:
    if arr.size > 20000:
       arr = arr[:20000]
    n = arr.size
    L = _pow2(2*n - 1)
    L2 = _pow2(3*n - 2)
    parent = _normalize(arr)
```

Full code draft:

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

def _pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()

def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    a /= s
    return a

def _fft_self(a, L):
    n = a.size
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    b = np.fft.irfft(F * F, L)
    b = b[: 2 * n - 1]
    if b.size:
        b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    return b

def _fast_score(a, L):
    S = a.sum()
    if S < 1e-12:
        return math.inf
    n = a.size
    b = _fft_self(a, L)
    return float(2.0 * n * b.max() / (S * S))

def _precompute_f(f, L, L2):
    return np.fft.rfft(f, L), np.fft.rfft(f[::-1], L2)

def _conv_f_x(FfL, x, L, n):
    Fx = np.fft.rfft(x, L)
    b = np.fft.irfft(FfL * Fx, L)
    b = b[: 2 * n - 1]
    return np.maximum(b, 0.0)

def _conv_y_f(FfrL2, y, L2, n):
    Fy = np.fft.rfft(y, L2)
    c = np.fft.irfft(Fy * FfrL2, L2)
    c = c[n - 1: 2 * n - 1]
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)

def _lp_step(FfL, FfrL2, x, alpha, power, n, L, L2, rng=None, floor=1e-15):
    b = _conv_f_x(FfL, x, L, n)
    maxb = b.max()
    if maxb <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    loads = b / maxb
    if power == 1.0:
        yp = loads
    else:
        yp = np.power(loads, power)
    ys = yp.sum()
    if ys <= 1e-300:
        return x
    y = yp / ys
    s = _conv_y_f(FfrL2, y, L2, n)
    smin = s.min()
    smax = s.max()
    rngv = smax - smin
    if rngv <= 1e-300:
        if rng is not None:
            x = x * (1.0 + 0.01 * rng.standard_normal(n))
            x = np.maximum(x, 1e-300)
        sx = x.sum()
        return x / sx if sx > 0 else np.full(n, 1.0 / n)
    u = (s - smin) / rngv
    x = x * np.exp(-alpha * u)
    x = np.maximum(x, floor)
    sx = x.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(sx) or sx <= 0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n)
    return x / sx

def _random_positive(rng, n):
    z = rng.standard_normal(n)
    Z = np.fft.rfft(z)
    cut = max(2, Z.size // 16)
    Z[cut:] = 0.0
    z = np.fft.irfft(Z, n)
    z -= z.mean()
    m = np.abs(z).max()
    if m > 0:
        z = z / m
    a = np.maximum(1.0 + 0.5 * z, 1e-6)
    return a / a.sum()

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    hard = t0 + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 12.0)

    try:
        arr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        arr = np.ones(6962, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = arr[np.isfinite(arr)]
    if arr.size < 128:
        arr = np.ones(6962, dtype=np.float64) if arr.size == 0 else arr
    if arr.size > 20000:
        arr = arr[:20000]
    n = int(arr.size)
    L = _pow2(2 * n - 1)
    L2 = _pow2(3 * n - 2)

    parent = _normalize(arr)
    best = parent.copy()
    best_score = _fast_score(best, L)
    if not math.isfinite(best_score):
        parent = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        best = parent.copy()
        best_score = 2.0

    if time.monotonic() > hard - 5:
        return [float(min(1000.0, max(0.0, x))) for x in best]

    def make_noise(amp):
        z = rng.standard_normal(n)
        Z = np.fft.rfft(z)
        cut = max(2, Z.size // 8)
        Z[cut:] = 0.0
        z = np.fft.irfft(Z, n)
        z = z - z[::-1]
        z -= z.mean()
        m = np.abs(z).max()
        if m > 0:
            z = z / m
        return amp * z

    candidates = [parent]
    for amp in (0.3, 0.6, 1.0):
        candidates.append(_normalize(parent + make_noise(amp)))
    scores = [_fast_score(c, L) for c in candidates]
    for i, sc in enumerate(scores):
        if sc + 1e-12 < best_score:
            best = candidates[i].copy()
            best_score = sc

    if np.var(parent) < 1e-12:
        idx = 1 + int(np.argmin(scores[1:]))
    else:
        idx = int(np.argmin(scores))
    current = candidates[idx].copy()
    x = _random_positive(rng, n)

    alphas = (0.6, 1.0, 1.6, 2.4)
    powers = (1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 2.0)
    ai = 1
    pi = 0
    alpha = alphas[ai]
    power = powers[pi]
    blend_ts = (0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0)
    batch = 1024 if n >= 3000 else 2048
    stagnant = 0
    floor = 1e-14 / max(1, n)

    while time.monotonic() < hard - 30:
        FfL, FfrL2 = _precompute_f(current, L, L2)
        for _ in range(batch):
            if time.monotonic() > hard - 30:
                break
            x = _lp_step(FfL, FfrL2, x, alpha, power, n, L, L2, rng, floor)
        g = _normalize(x)
        cur_score = _fast_score(current, L)
        best_cand = current
        best_cand_score = cur_score
        for tt in blend_ts:
            if tt >= 1.0:
                cand = g
            else:
                cand = (1.0 - tt) * current + tt * g
            cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
            cs = cand.sum()
            if cs <= 1e-12:
                continue
            cand /= cs
            sc = _fast_score(cand, L)
            if sc + 1e-12 < best_cand_score:
                best_cand_score = sc
                best_cand = cand
        if best_cand_score + 1e-12 < best_score:
            best = best_cand.copy()
            best_score = best_cand_score
        if best_cand_score + 1e-12 < cur_score:
            current = best_cand
            stagnant = 0
        else:
            stagnant += 1
            if stagnant >= 3:
                base = best if (best_score < cur_score - 0.02) else current
                current = _normalize(base + 0.4 * make_noise(1.0))
                x = _random_positive(rng, n)
                ai = (ai + 1) % len(alphas)
                pi = (pi + 1) % len(powers)
                alpha = alphas[ai]
                power = powers[pi]
                stagnant = 0

    # Final exact validation using supplied evaluator.
    final_best = best
    final_score = math.inf
    trial_candidates = [best, parent, current]
    if time.monotonic() < hard - 2:
        for cand in trial_candidates:
            if cand is None or cand.size == 0:
                continue
            try:
                val = evaluate_sequence([float(v) for v in cand])
            except Exception:
                val = math.inf
            if math.isfinite(val) and val < final_score:
                final_score = val
                final_best = cand
    out = [float(min(1000.0, max(0.0, x))) for x in final_best]
    return out
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math


def _pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    a /= s
    return a


def _fft_self(a, L):
    n = a.size
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    b = np.fft.irfft(F * F, L)
    b = b[: 2 * n - 1]
    if b.size:
        b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    return b


def _fast_score(a, L):
    S = a.sum()
    if S < 1e-12:
        return math.inf
    n = a.size
    b = _fft_self(a, L)
    return float(2.0 * n * b.max() / (S * S))


def _precompute_f(f, L, L2):
    return np.fft.rfft(f, L), np.fft.rfft(f[::-1], L2)


def _conv_f_x(FfL, x, L, n):
    Fx = np.fft.rfft(x, L)
    b = np.fft.irfft(FfL * Fx, L)
    b = b[: 2 * n - 1]
    return np.maximum(b, 0.0)


def _conv_y_f(FfrL2, y, L2, n):
    Fy = np.fft.rfft(y, L2)
    c = np.fft.irfft(Fy * FfrL2, L2)
    c = c[n - 1: 2 * n - 1]
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def _lp_step(FfL, FfrL2, x, alpha, power, n, L, L2, rng=None, floor=1e-15):
    b = _conv_f_x(FfL, x, L, n)
    maxb = b.max()
    if maxb <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)

    loads = b / maxb
    if power == 1.0:
        yp = loads
    else:
        yp = np.power(loads, power)

    ys = yp.sum()
    if ys <= 1e-300:
        return x

    y = yp / ys
    s = _conv_y_f(FfrL2, y, L2, n)
    smin = s.min()
    smax = s.max()
    span = smax - smin

    if span <= 1e-300:
        if rng is not None:
            x = x * (1.0 + 0.01 * rng.standard_normal(n))
            x = np.maximum(x, 1e-300)
        sx = x.sum()
        if sx > 0 and math.isfinite(sx):
            return x / sx
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)

    u = (s - smin) / span
    x = x * np.exp(-alpha * u)
    x = np.maximum(x, floor)
    sx = x.sum()
    if not math.isfinite(sx) or sx <= 0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return x / sx


def _random_positive(rng, n):
    z = rng.standard_normal(n)
    Z = np.fft.rfft(z)
    cut = max(2, Z.size // 16)
    Z[cut:] = 0.0
    z = np.fft.irfft(Z, n)
    z -= z.mean()
    m = np.abs(z).max()
    if m > 0:
        z = z / m
    a = np.maximum(1.0 + 0.5 * z, 1e-6)
    return a / a.sum()


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    hard = t0 + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 12.0)

    try:
        arr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        arr = np.ones(6962, dtype=np.float64)

    arr = arr[np.isfinite(arr)]
    if arr.size < 128:
        arr = np.ones(6962, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size > 20000:
        arr = arr[:20000]

    n = int(arr.size)
    L = _pow2(2 * n - 1)
    L2 = _pow2(3 * n - 2)

    parent = _normalize(arr)
    best = parent.copy()
    best_score = _fast_score(best, L)
    if not math.isfinite(best_score):
        parent = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        best = parent.copy()
        best_score = 2.0

    if time.monotonic() > hard - 5:
        return [float(min(1000.0, max(0.0, x))) for x in best]

    def make_noise(amp):
        z = rng.standard_normal(n)
        Z = np.fft.rfft(z)
        cut = max(2, Z.size // 8)
        Z[cut:] = 0.0
        z = np.fft.irfft(Z, n)
        z = z - z[::-1]
        z -= z.mean()
        m = np.abs(z).max()
        if m > 0:
            z = z / m
        return amp * z

    candidates = [parent]
    for amp in (0.05, 0.15, 0.35, 0.7):
        candidates.append(_normalize(parent + make_noise(amp)))

    scores = [_fast_score(c, L) for c in candidates]
    for i, sc in enumerate(scores):
        if sc + 1e-12 < best_score:
            best = candidates[i].copy()
            best_score = sc

    if np.var(parent) < 1e-12:
        idx = 1 + int(np.argmin(scores[1:]))
    else:
        idx = int(np.argmin(scores))

    current = candidates[idx].copy()
    x = _random_positive(rng, n)

    alphas = (0.6, 1.0, 1.6, 2.4)
    powers = (1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 2.0)
    ai = 1
    pi = 0
    alpha = alphas[ai]
    power = powers[pi]

    blend_ts = (0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0)
    batch = 1024 if n >= 3000 else 2048
    stagnant = 0
    floor = 1e-14 / max(1, n)

    while time.monotonic() < hard - 30:
        FfL, FfrL2 = _precompute_f(current, L, L2)

        for _ in range(batch):
            if time.monotonic() > hard - 30:
                break
            x = _lp_step(FfL, FfrL2, x, alpha, power, n, L, L2, rng, floor)

        g = _normalize(x)
        cur_score = _fast_score(current, L)

        best_cand = current
        best_cand_score = cur_score

        for tt in blend_ts:
            if tt >= 1.0:
                cand = g
            else:
                cand = (1.0 - tt) * current + tt * g

            cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
            cs = cand.sum()
            if cs <= 1e-12:
                continue
            cand /= cs

            sc = _fast_score(cand, L)
            if sc + 1e-12 < best_cand_score:
                best_cand_score = sc
                best_cand = cand

        if best_cand_score + 1e-12 < best_score:
            best = best_cand.copy()
            best_score = best_cand_score

        if best_cand_score + 1e-12 < cur_score:
            current = best_cand
            stagnant = 0
        else:
            stagnant += 1
            if stagnant >= 3:
                base = best if (best_score < cur_score - 0.02) else current
                current = _normalize(base + 0.4 * make_noise(1.0))
                x = _random_positive(rng, n)
                ai = (ai + 1) % len(alphas)
                pi = (pi + 1) % len(powers)
                alpha = alphas[ai]
                power = powers[pi]
                stagnant = 0

    final_best = best
    final_score = math.inf

    if n <= 12000 and time.monotonic() < hard - 2:
        trial_candidates = [best, parent, current]
        for cand in trial_candidates:
            if cand is None or cand.size == 0:
                continue
            try:
                val = evaluate_sequence([float(v) for v in cand])
            except Exception:
                val = math.inf
            if math.isfinite(val) and val < final_score:
                final_score = val
                final_best = cand

    return [float(min(1000.0, max(0.0, x))) for x in final_best]
```
